Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The modular operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 169. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 314 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_169(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 169. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^314(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the modular structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 872. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 106 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 872 + O(1/n^106). Summing over all modes and applying the modular structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
